Second, adding regions sharing a common price path reduces outcome noise but does not create independent price trajectories; conversely, averaging across units with independent design-specific errors reduces dispersion at the standard square root rate. Third, a Paule-Mandel variance component estimated across independently priced units substantially increases empirical coverage in homogeneous simulations, from 0.469 to 0.931. The broader implication is a shift toward designing data-generating processes that create independent identifying variation rather than relying solely on fixed passive panels.

</details>


### [210] [Asymmetric Capacity Allocation in Self-Refinement Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21345)
*Zhuoyi Yang,Ian G. Harris,Salar Hashemitaheri,Cassie Huang,Yuangang Li,Hyunwoo Oh,Paul Dourish,Tony Givargis,Mohsen Imani,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了自精炼（self-refinement）流水线中生成、批评、修改三个阶段对模型规模的不同敏感性，发现更大的生成器和修改器能提升效果，而过小的修改器甚至会损害性能；批评器的大小对性能影响极不敏感，但即使很小的批评器也优于完全省略批评环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自精炼（生成-批评-修改）是提升LLM生成质量的广泛使用的范式，也是许多LLM智能体的核心机制。然而，以往研究大多将模型大小视为实现细节而非研究对象，忽视了三个阶段不同的认知需求，可能导致资源浪费。缺乏对模型规模如何影响各阶段以及是否需要同等能力的模型来完成各阶段的系统研究。

Method: 首次对各阶段进行模型规模的逐级（stage-wise）对照研究：在5个不同领域的基准上，使用Qwen3的6种模型规模和Gemma 3的4种模型规模，系统地比较了不同规模组合下的生成器、批评器和修改器对自精炼流水线整体性能的影响。

Result: （1）更大的生成器和修改器普遍提升流水线性能，但过小的修改器甚至可能损害性能；（2）批评器的大小对性能极为不敏感，但即便是很小的批评器也比完全省略批评环节表现更好。

Conclusion: 模型能力不应在自精炼流水线的各阶段中均匀分配，不同阶段表现出截然不同的规模缩放特性。这为设计更计算高效的多阶段语言模型系统提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Self-refinement, typically structured as generation, critique, and revision, is a widely adopted paradigm for improving LLM generation and serves as a core mechanism in many LLM agents. While the three stages involve different cognitive demands, most existing approaches conveniently treat the model size as an implementation detail rather than a subject of study, which may lead to a waste of resources. Little work has systematically examined how model size affects each stage or whether effective self-refinement requires equally capable models for generation, critique, and revision. We present the first stage-wise model size study of the self-refinement pipeline on 5 benchmarks from different domains using 6 model sizes of Qwen3 and 4 model sizes of Gemma 3. We conclude that larger generators and refiners generally improve the pipeline, whereas an undersized refiner can even harm performance. Second, performance is highly insensitive to the size of the critic, although including even a small critic consistently outperforms omitting critique altogether. Our findings demonstrate that model capacity should not be allocated uniformly across self-refinement pipelines. Instead, different stages exhibit distinct size scaling characteristics, providing practical guidance for designing more computationally efficient multi-stage language model systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Minimax Quantile Bounds via Information Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20857)
*Amedeo Roberto Esposito*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的信息论框架，用于对极小极大分位数进行下界估计。其核心是损失自适应的Neyman–Pearson元逆否命题，通过优化辅助输出分布，该框架将先验和损失诱导的小球几何与实验的统计可区分性分离。不同松弛方式分别导出基于f-信息度、Sibson信息、最大泄漏和Amemiya范数的逆否命题，Fano和Le Cam下界均为特例。框架进一步阐释了不同信息度量适用于不同恢复准则的原因，并在高斯加权随机块模型、低秩矩阵估计、近似Hamming恢复和Poisson定位等任务中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有极小极大下界方法（如Fano、Le Cam）在不同恢复准则下表现不一，缺乏一个统一框架来理解何时该用何种信息度量，以及如何获得sharp的极小极大分位数量级。本文旨在建立统一框架并阐明不同信息测度与恢复准则之间的匹配关系。

Method: 提出损失自适应的Neyman–Pearson元逆否命题作为起点，该界将小球几何（由先验与损失决定）与实验统计可区分性分离，并对辅助输出分布进行优化。通过对该单一检验界进行不同松弛，分别导出基于f-信息度、Sibson信息I_α、最大泄漏和Amemiya范数的逆否命题；Fano与Le Cam界作为特例恢复。

Result: 在平衡高斯加权随机块模型精确恢复问题中，借助最大泄漏恒等式获得有限样本的极小极大精确恢复风险界；在低秩矩阵估计中对各向同性有界能量噪声给出有限样本的极小极大分位数双侧界。在异构二值模型中证明优化有限Sibson阶能给出强逆，而最大泄漏特化是平凡的；在一坐标Poisson定位中，通过Amemiya范数使用的Bennett型Young函数恢复了精确的成功概率尺度，而经典Fano和固定幂松弛严格更弱。

Conclusion: Sharp的极小极大分位数逆否命题需要根据恢复分辨率与似然比尾部来适配信息控制工具。不同信息度量对不同恢复准则存在本质性匹配关系——最大泄漏适用于对称精确恢复，有限阶Sibson信息适用于近似Hamming恢复，Amemiya范数类Young函数适用于重尾似然比场景。该框架统一了经典下界方法，并为选择合适信息测度提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We develop a unified information-theoretic framework for lower bounding minimax quantiles. The starting point is a loss-adapted Neyman--Pearson metaconverse that bounds the minimax success probability at every loss radius and confidence level. The bound separates the small-ball geometry induced by the prior and loss from the statistical distinguishability of the experiment, and is optimised over an auxiliary output distribution. Different relaxations of this single testing bound yield converses based on \(f\)-informativity, Sibson information \(I_α\), Maximal Leakage, and Amemiya norms. Classical Fano and Le Cam lower bounds are recovered as special cases.
  The framework also clarifies why different information measures are suited to different recovery criteria. Maximal Leakage is exact for a class of symmetric exact-recovery problems. We use this identity to derive finite-sample bounds on the full minimax exact-recovery risk in the balanced Gaussian weighted stochastic block model, as well as two-sided finite-sample minimax-quantile bounds for low-rank matrix estimation under isotropic bounded-energy noise. For approximate Hamming recovery, we exhibit a heterogeneous binary model in which an optimised finite Sibson order yields a strong converse while the Maximal Leakage specialisation is trivial. Finally, for one-coordinate Poisson localisation, a Bennett-type Young function used through its Amemiya norm recovers the exact success-probability scale, whereas classical Fano and fixed-power relaxations are strictly weaker. These results show that sharp minimax-quantile converses require adapting the information control to both the recovery resolution and the likelihood-ratio tail.

</details>


### [212] [Fluid-Antenna-Assisted Distributed Joint Decoding for Cell-Free Massive MIMO Unsourced Random Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20885)
*Liandong Hu,Jian Dang,Zaichen Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种流体天线辅助的分布式联合译码器，用于无小区大规模MIMO下的无源随机接入（URA）。该方法通过单射频链接入点顺序探测相关端口，利用相关性感知的期望最大化近似消息传递算法，克服了多用户干扰、分布式信道不确定性和局部衰落等限制，相比固定端口方案在平均接收功率和低尾性能上均有提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无源随机接入（URA）支持海量稀疏连接，但受限于多用户干扰、分布式信道不确定性和局部衰落。现有方案难以同时缓解这些挑战，需要一种新的框架来提升URA在无小区大规模MIMO场景下的性能。

Method: 1) 接入点采用单射频链顺序探测相关端口（流体天线技术）；2) 中央处理器执行相关性感知的期望最大化近似消息传递（EM-AMP）算法；3) 每个接入点选择一个共同的载荷端口；4) 结合MIMO迭代高斯近似、全包信道重估和连续干扰消除（SIC）；5) 通过固定端口消融实验和流体天线仿真进行验证。

Result: 固定端口消融实验证实了分布式接收、信道重估和迭代消除的互补收益；流体天线仿真表明其平均接收功率和低尾接收功率均优于固定端口接收方案。

Conclusion: 该框架为流体天线与无小区URA的集成提供了可行方案，并激励在导频碰撞和切换开销条件下进行端到端评估。

Abstract: Unsourced random access (URA) supports massive sporadic connectivity but remains limited by multiuser interference, distributed channel uncertainty, and local fading. This paper proposes a fluid-antenna-assisted distributed joint decoder for cell-free massive MIMO URA. Single-radio-frequencychain access points sequentially sound correlated ports; the central processor then performs correlation-aware expectationmaximization approximate message passing, selects one common payload port per access point, and combines MIMO iterative Gaussian approximation with full-packet channel re-estimation and successive interference cancellation. Fixed-port ablations confirm the complementary benefits of distributed reception, reestimation, and iterative cancellation, while fluid-antenna simulations show improved average and lower-tail received power over fixed-port reception. The framework provides a feasible integration of fluid antennas and cell-free URA and motivates end-to-end evaluation under pilot collisions and switching overhead.

</details>


### [213] [The first tight classification of skew-constacyclic codes over finite fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21339)
*Monica Nevins,Susanne Pumluen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文通过分类有限域上斜常循环码的环（Petit环）的等距类与等价类，实现了对斜常循环码的等距类与等价类的参数化，并给出了相应的算法及等价类计数，同时提供了大量等距但不等价的示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斜常循环码是有限域上一类重要的编码，但其等距类和等价类的参数化问题尚未得到系统解决。由于Hamming权重保持的同构（等距）是比等价更精细的关系，需要对所有可能的Hamming权重保持同构进行考虑，才能实现紧致（tight）的分类。

Method: 通过将斜常循环码与其环境Petit环联系起来，对Petit环在Hamming权重保持同构意义下的等距类和等价类进行分类。通过参数化这些环的类来间接参数化斜常循环码的类，并提出用于该参数化的算法。

Result: 给出了有限域上斜常循环码等距类和等价类的完整参数化方法及算法，计算了这些环的等价类数量，并构造了大量等距但不等价的码示例。

Conclusion: 该工作表明等距是严格强于等价的关系，即存在许多码在Hamming权重保持同构下等距但并非等价；通过Petit环分类提供的参数化方法为斜常循环码的精细分类提供了系统工具。

Abstract: We parametrize the isometry and equivalence classes of skew constacyclic codes over a finite field by classifying the corresponding classes of their ambient rings, and present algorithms for the parametrizations. We achieve a tight classification by taking all possible Hamming-weight preserving isomorphisms between their ambient Petit rings into account. We also count the number of equivalence classes of these rings. We present many examples where isometry is a strictly stronger relation than equivalence, that is, codes that are isometric but not equivalent.

</details>

### [214] [An Imaging-Informed Reaction-Diffusion Model of Infarct Growth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20935)
*Muhammad Hussnain Abbas,Michal Balcerak,Asif Ahmad,Bjoern Menze,Ezequiel de la Rosa*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文首次提出从临床急性MRI直接参数化Fisher-KPP反应-扩散偏微分方程的影像驱动框架，用于预测最终缺血性梗死体积。在ISLES 2017子集（N=29）上采用oracle框架评估，物理约束模型平均Dice为0.46±0.24，显著优于标准rCBF阈值法的0.25±0.21，并通过消融实验验证了异质扩散场建模对半暗带扩展预测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测最终缺血性梗死体积对个体化卒中管理至关重要，但现有策略存在两极分化：不可解释的机器学习架构与过于详细、在临床影像场景中难以处理的电生理模型。作者旨在弥合这一临床鸿沟，提出首个能从临床急性MRI直接参数化PDE的影像驱动框架。

Method: 将连续状态变量u(x,t)∈[0,1]建模组织损伤，捕捉离子应激通过细胞外空间的前向扩展，以及基线灌注缺损（Tmax>6s）内受门控的局部代谢性细胞死亡承诺。从临床急性MRI直接参数化Fisher-KPP反应-扩散PDE，并在ISLES 2017数据集子集（N=29）上通过oracle框架评估。

Result: 物理约束模型在ISLES 2017子集上取得平均Dice分数0.46±0.24，而标准rCBF阈值法仅为0.25±0.21。消融实验表明，建模源于临床灌注图的时空异质扩散场能准确捕获半暗带扩展，同时提供完整的生理可解释性。

Conclusion: 该概念验证确立了一阶物理原理能够直接在临床扫描网格上捕获复杂缺血进展，将范式从纯数据驱动模型转向患者特异性生物物理预测。

Abstract: Predicting final ischemic infarct volumes from acute imaging is a cornerstone of personalized stroke management, yet current strategies remain polarized between uninterpretable machine learning architectures and overly detailed electrophysiological models that are intractable in clinical imaging settings. We bridge this clinical gap by introducing the first imaging-driven framework that parameterizes a Fisher-KPP reaction-diffusion partial differential equation (PDE) directly from clinical acute MRI. The continuous state variable $u(\mathbf{x},t)\in[0,1]$ models tissue damage, capturing the forward expansion of ionic stress through the extracellular space alongside a localized metabolic commitment to cell death gated within the baseline perfusion deficit ($T_{\max}>6$\,s). Evaluating this paradigm on a subset of the ISLES 2017 dataset ($N=29$) via an oracle framework reveals that incorporating biophysical propagation constraints yields a mean Dice score of $0.46 \pm 0.24$, compared to standard rCBF thresholding ($0.25 \pm 0.21$). Extensive ablations demonstrate that modeling spatially heterogeneous diffusion fields derived from clinical perfusion maps accurately captures penumbral expansion, while providing full physiological interpretability. This proof-of-concept establishes that first-principles physics could capture complex ischemic progression directly on clinical scan grids, shifting the paradigm from purely data-driven models toward patient-specific biophysical forecasting.

</details>


### [215] [Broadband Stable Calderón-Preconditioned Vector-Potential-Only Integral Equations for PEC Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20993)
*Paul Olyslager,Hendrik Rogier,Kristof Cools*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出一种稳定的第一类Calderón预条件矢量势积分方程方法，用于完美电导体散射问题。该方法通过后处理中的Lorenz规范计算标量势，在任意低频下都能准确计算近场和远场，且不受机器精度和求积精度限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对完美电导体散射的低频问题，传统的积分方程方法在低频下会遇到数值不稳定性问题，特别是矢量势和标量势的计算精度会严重退化。现有方法往往依赖于机器精度或求积精度来缓解这些问题，无法在任意低频下保持准确性。

Method: 1) 首先研究自由度以及近场和远场的低频缩放规律；2) 基于低频缩放推导稳定化方法；3) 利用准Helmholtz投影子稳定化矢量势旋度的切向迹；4) 利用基于静态标量势的投影子稳定化矢量势的法向迹；5) 在后处理步骤中通过Lorenz规范计算标量势。

Result: 数值实验表明，所提出的稳定化方法在极低频率下仍能获得准确的结果，近场和远场均可精确计算，且精度不依赖于机器精度和求积精度。

Conclusion: 该方法成功实现了任意低频下完美电导体散射的稳定、高精度求解，矢量势和标量势均能保持准确性，且通过后处理独立于机器和求积精度，为低频电磁散射问题提供了鲁棒的数值方案。

Abstract: We propose a stabilized first-kind, Calderón preconditioned, vector-potential-only integral equation, for scattering at perfect electrical conductors. The method yields accurate low-frequency results for both the vector and the scalar potential, where the scalar potential is computed using the Lorenz gauge in the post-processing. Both the near and far fields can be accurately computed at arbitrarily low frequencies. This is achieved in a post-processing step and is independent of machine and quadrature precision. First, the low-frequency scaling of the degrees of freedom, and the near and far fields, are studied. Next, stabilized methods are derived based on the low-frequency scaling. The tangential trace of the curl of the vector-potential is stabilized leveraging quasi-Helmholtz projectors, while the normal trace of the vector-potential is stabilized with a projector based on the static scalar potential. Numerical experiments are presented showing that the stabilized method shows accurate results even for very low frequencies.

</details>


### [216] [Techno-Economic Analysis of Repurposing Abandoned Oil Wells for Geothermal Energy Extraction Using Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21092)
*Hung-Yu Lin,Kuan-Chun Shih,Lea-Der Chen*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于物理信息神经网络（PINN）的模型，用于评估闭环地热系统（CLGS）在改造闲置油井中的性能。该模型在稀疏训练数据下准确预测了25年的井筒温度和流动特征，耦合有机朗肯循环（ORC）后热力学效率达9.5%，并通过DPP、NPV和LCOE等经济指标验证了投资可行性，为加速地热能源采用提供了可扩展且物理一致的评估工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现2050年净零排放目标，需要多元化可再生能源。地热能相比于水电、风能和太阳能仍未得到充分利用，而传统增强型地热系统（EGS）依赖水力刺激，存在诱发地震等环境风险。因此需要一种安全、无环境危害的替代方案来开发利用地热资源。

Method: 开发了物理信息神经网络（PINN）模型，将热传导和对流等物理方程显式嵌入神经网络学习过程中，以建模闭环地热系统（CLGS）性能。与传统神经网络不同，PINN在训练中融入物理约束，可在稀疏数据条件下准确预测井筒温度和流动特征。模型进一步与有机朗肯循环（ORC）耦合评估热能转换效率，并进行经济指标分析（DPP、NPV、LCOE）。

Result: PINN模型在25年时间跨度内实现了稳定的长期预测，精确捕捉了井筒温度和流动特征。耦合ORC后系统热力学效率达到9.5%。经济性评估表明该系统在投资可行性方面具有潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为评估地热系统的技术性能和经济回报提供了可扩展、物理一致的工具有效路径，为加速地热能源的广泛应用和闲置油井的再利用提供了稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: To achieve net-zero targets by 2050, it is critical to diversify renewable energy. Hydropower, wind, and solar energy dominate; geothermal energy remains underutilized. Conventional Enhanced Geothermal Systems (EGS) rely on hydraulic stimulation, which poses risks such as induced seismicity. To address this, Closed-Loop Geothermal Systems (CLGS) circulate working fluids in sealed tubing to avoid direct reservoir contact, making them a potential solution for repurposing idle oil wells without environmental hazards. This study developed a Physics-Informed Neural Network (PINN) to model the CLGS performance. Unlike traditional neural networks, PINN explicitly embeds governing physical equations into their learning processes, such as heat conduction and convection. This integration enabled the model to accurately predict the wellbore temperature and flow characteristics over a 25-year lifespan, even with sparse training data. The simulation results confirmed stable long-term predictions. When coupled with an Organic Rankine Cycle (ORC) model, the system yielded a thermodynamic efficiency of 9.5%. Crucially, several economic indicators (e.g., DPP, NPV, and LCOE) are conducted to evaluate the investment feasibility and economic potential of the proposed CLGS-based power generation system. This proposed framework provides a scalable, physics-consistent tool for evaluating both technical performance and economic returns, offering a robust pathway to accelerate geothermal adoption.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Maximum Entropy Encoding of Energy-Weighted Spherical Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20429)
*Jiaze Sun*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用有限矩（总能量、一阶方向矩、无迹二阶矩）压缩和重建由非负Monte Carlo路径样本组成的角能量信号用于辐照度计算。通过最大熵闭包得到p(r,u) ∝ exp(-βr g(u))的解析形式，其中g(u)方向概率密度与g⁻³成正比、角能量密度与g⁻⁴成正比。在981个Poly Haven HDRI 2K场景和三个Debevec探针上验证，五参数MaxEnt在每场景胜率上达到78.7%（对比存储QZH），平均亮度RMSE降低15.8%，且所有场景保持零负辐照度。全二阶SH-2总体误差最低，五参数MaxEnt排名第二但在强方向性场景中优于SH-2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的辐照度环境映射方法（如SH、QZH）在压缩角能量信号时可能产生负辐照度或精度不足的问题。作者希望利用由非负Monte Carlo路径样本组成的角能量信号的统计特性，通过有限矩（可线性累加、旋转协变）进行压缩，并借助最大熵原理得到解析可重建的闭合形式，从而在保持物理有效性的同时提高重建精度。

Method: 采用总能量（1维）、一阶方向矩（3维）和无迹二阶矩（5维）作为1+3+5共9维线性可加、旋转协变的统计量。在固定Lebesgue参考测度下应用最大熵闭包，推导出p(r,u) ∝ exp(-βr g(u))的形式，其中g(u) = 1 - b·u + u^T Q u，方向概率密度∝g⁻³，角能量密度∝g⁻⁴。对纯偶极四参数子族提供了解析矩匹配、方差、逆采样和闭式漫反射响应；对偶极-二阶矩共轴五参数子族提供了可实现域、配分函数、方位角代数积分及面向LUT的重建形式。

Result: 在981个Poly Haven HDRI 2K场景和三个Debevec探针上实验：五参数MaxEnt对存储QZH的每场景胜率为78.7%，平均亮度RMSE下降15.8%，且在强方向性场景中优势更显著；两种MaxEnt变体在所有场景中保持零负辐照度。全二阶SH-2总体误差最低，五参数MaxEnt排名第二但在高方向性桶中超过SH-2。共轴子族在非共轴多光源场景中表现出系统性闭包误差。

Conclusion: 最大熵闭包为角能量信号的有限矩压缩与重建提供了一种物理有效（严格非负）且精度高的方法。五参数MaxEnt在大多数场景中优于存储QZH，并在强方向性场景中胜过SH-2，尽管全二阶SH-2在总体上仍具最低误差。共轴子族对非共轴多光源场景存在系统性闭包误差，提示需要更高阶参数来完整建模。

Abstract: We study how angular energy signals composed of non-negative Monte Carlo path samples can be compressed and reconstructed for irradiance using finite moments. Writing each sample as an energy-weighted directional feature $x = r u$, we adopt total energy, the first directional moment, and the traceless second moment as $1+3+5$ linearly additive, rotationally covariant statistics. Under a fixed Lebesgue reference measure, the maximum-entropy closure yields $p(r,u) \propto \exp(-βr g(u))$, where $g(u) = 1 - b \cdot u + u^T Q u$, whose directional probability and angular energy density are proportional to $g^{-3}$ and $g^{-4}$, respectively. When $g_{\min} > 0$ the closure is normalizable and the reconstruction is strictly positive. We further provide analytic moment matching, variance, inverse sampling, and closed-form diffuse response for the pure-dipole four-parameter subfamily, as well as the realizability domain, partition function, azimuthal algebraic integral, and LUT-oriented reconstruction form for the dipole-second-moment coaxial five-parameter subfamily. Experiments cover 981 Poly Haven HDRI 2K scenes and three Debevec probes. Five-parameter MaxEnt achieves a 78.7% per-scene win rate against stored QZH, with mean luminance RMSE reduced by 15.8%; the advantage is more pronounced in scenes with strong directionality. Both MaxEnt variants maintain zero negative irradiance across all scenes. Full second-order SH-2 yields the lowest overall error, while five-parameter MaxEnt ranks second and outperforms SH-2 in the high-directionality bucket; the coaxial subfamily shows systematic closure error on non-coaxial multi-source scenes.

</details>


### [218] [MultiCube: Compositional 3D Generation With Part-Level Semantic and Spatial Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20448)
*Ava Pun,Kangle Deng,Yiheng Zhu,Jun-Yan Zhu,Maneesh Agrawala,Tinghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: MultiCube是一种新颖的组合式3D生成方法，通过全局文本提示、文本模式（指定各个部件）和空间布局（各部件边界框）作为输入，输出由独立网格组成的3D对象。该方法采用两阶段扩散过程，先生成模式与布局对齐的整体网格，再同时分解为各个部件，并通过新颖的Part Layout Adapter独立编码每个部件的条件，实现精确的部件级语义和空间控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的3D生成方法虽然能基于图像或文本提示生成高质量的组合式对象，但这类全局条件缺乏专业创意工作流所需的精确部件级可控性。用户无法独立控制每个部分的语义和空间排列，这限制了在游戏和动画等专业场景中的实际应用。

Method: MultiCube接受三种输入：全局文本提示、指定期望部件的文本模式、指示部件边界框的空间布局。方法采用两阶段扩散过程：(1) 先生成与模式及布局对齐的整体网格；(2) 然后同时将网格分解为各个独立部件。方法引入了新颖的Part Layout Adapter，用于独立于其他部件地编码每个部件的条件信息。

Result: 实验表明，MultiCube能够生成高质量的组合式3D对象，并实现精确的部件级控制，包括那些仅通过文本或图像提示难以实现的独特布局。

Conclusion: MultiCube显著推进了3D生成中的部件级可控性，为专业创意工作流提供了独立的语义和空间控制能力，弥补了全局条件生成方法在精细控制方面的不足。

Abstract: Digital 3D objects used in games and animation are often required to be compositional; that is, decomposed into semantically meaningful parts. Recent 3D generation methods can produce high-quality compositional objects conditioned on image or text prompts. Yet, such global conditioning lacks the precise part-level controllability required for professional creative workflows. To address this, we introduce MultiCube, a novel compositional 3D generation method that provides explicit, independent control over both the semantics and spatial arrangement of each part. MultiCube takes as input a global text prompt, a text schema specifying the desired parts, and a spatial layout indicating the bounding boxes of the parts in the given schema. It outputs a 3D object composed of distinct meshes, one per specified part, that adhere to the given semantic and spatial conditions. Our approach employs a two-stage diffusion process, first generating a schema- and layout-aligned monolithic mesh, then decomposing the mesh into individual parts simultaneously. A novel Part Layout Adapter is used to encode per-part conditions independently of the other parts. Experiments demonstrate that our method can generate high-quality compositional 3D objects with precise part-level control, including those with unique layouts difficult to achieve with text or image prompting alone. Project page: https://multi-cube.github.io

</details>


### [219] [CubicSplat: Differentiable Vector Graphics via Error-Bounded Forward Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20803)
*Chenglong Liu,Xin Zhang,Yimeng Zhu,Liyang He,Yixiao Ma,Yu Su,Zhenya Huang,Qi Liu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Vector graphics are prized for their resolution independence, compact storage, and direct editability, making differentiable optimization of their parametric primitives an attractive goal. Yet classical rasterization is discontinuous with respect to geometry, and existing remedies that smooth the forward pass demand increasingly elaborate heuristics as scene complexity grows. We trace this fragility to a gradient seesaw: design choices that improve forward geometric exactness can systematically degrade the induced gradient signal, and vice versa. To navigate this tension we introduce CubicSplat, a differentiable vector rasterizer that replaces Bézier closest-point solvers with uniform polyline surrogates whose geometric error is bounded at $O(S^{-2})$. The resulting static computation graph yields well-conditioned gradients by construction, while a compositing-derived visibility mechanism prunes degenerate primitives without auxiliary regularization. On DIV2K and Kodak benchmarks CubicSplat achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality with over 2 dB PSNR gain in the closed-fill setting, while training up to 4x faster than prior methods. The code is available at https://github.com/CubicSplat/repo

</details>

### [220] [SDAD: Spec-Driven Agentic Development for the AI-Native SDLC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20341)
*Vu Hung Nguyen,Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD)"形式化框架，将面向大语言模型的智能编码代理与上游规范精度相结合，作为自主软件交付的第四种生产范式，并提出了团队角色演变、量化治理指标及分阶段采用蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拥有十万至百万级token上下文窗口的前沿编码代理正在重构软件开发生命周期（SDLC），规范质量成为自主交付的执行燃料。本文旨在形式化一种将严谨的上游规范形式化与高速实现相综合的新开发范式，回应"代理速度是否消除工程纪律"这一核心问题。

Method: (1) 综合瀑布与敏捷的历史辩证，提出SDAD五环节流程（意图捕获、机器可读规范、代理综合、独立多代理验证、人类签核）；(2) 引入"AI代码"作为第四生产范式；(3) 对比2020年Human-Agile与2026年Agentic-SDAD在制品、节奏、问责制、安全姿态上的差异；(4) 构建量化治理指标体系（歧义税、规范保真度、SER、带修复乘数的TCI_agentic）；(5) 提出混合估算与分阶段迁移蓝图；(6) 整合工业与研究证据以论证综合权与发布权的分离。

Result: 论文提出了一套完整的SDAD理论框架，包括范式对比模型、角色转变模型、量化治理体系（含Ambiguity Tax、Spec Fidelity、SER、TCI_agentic及修复乘数phi）以及混合估算和分阶段迁移的实用采用路线。基于AI增强测试与验证的工业和研究证据，论证了合成权威与发布权威分离的必要性。

Conclusion: 代理速度并不消除工程纪律，而是将纪律向上游转移——转移到规范精度、显式门控和可审计溯源之中。SDAD将严格的工程设计原则重新定位为自主代理时代的核心竞争力，规范质量成为交付速度的先行条件而非可牺牲项。

Abstract: Frontier coding agents backed by large language models with context windows from hundreds of thousands to millions of tokens are restructuring the Software Development Life Cycle (SDLC). Rich context handling and multi-step reasoning now allow substantial Functional Requirement Documents (FRDs) and repository context to be ingested in a single workflow, making specification quality the execution fuel for autonomous delivery. This report formalises Spec-Driven Agentic Development (SDAD) as a synthesis of disciplined up-front formalisation and high-velocity implementation: intent capture, machine-readable specification, agentic synthesis, and independent multi-agent verification under human sign-off. We revisit the historical pendulum between Waterfall and Agile, introduce AI-code as a fourth production paradigm, and compare Human-Agile (circa 2020) with Agentic-SDAD (circa 2026) across artefacts, cadence, accountability, and security posture. Beyond process description, we extend the model to team role metamorphosis (engineer, QA, platform, and product functions), quantitative governance (Ambiguity Tax, Spec Fidelity, SER, and TCI_agentic with repair multiplier phi), and pragmatic adoption via hybrid estimation and a staged migration blueprint. Industrial and research evidence on AI-augmented testing and verification is integrated to motivate separation between synthesis and release authority. Overall, the paper argues that agentic speed does not eliminate engineering discipline; it relocates discipline upstream into specification precision, explicit gates, and auditable provenance.

</details>


### [221] [PrimeAgentOrchestrator: Memory-Primed Agent Spawning for Personal AI Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20342)
*Myron Koch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO)，一个为LLM编码智能体注入持久记忆的系统。PAO在Claude Code启动时并行查询PostgreSQL实体-观察数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引两个独立记忆后端，融合结果后通过文件系统注入将记忆简报提供给智能体。作者报告了四个月（2025年12月至2026年3月）的部署经验，记录了三代上下文传递机制的演进、各代的重构动机及工程权衡，最终选择桥接异构记忆系统而非构建统一存储。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码智能体每次会话都以空上下文窗口开始，丢弃了之前工作中积累的知识，导致无法利用跨会话的累积经验。作者旨在通过为智能体会话启动时注入相关记忆来解决这一长期记忆缺失问题。

Method: PAO在智能体生成时并行查询两个独立运行的内存后端（PostgreSQL实体-观察数据库和Cloudflare Worker语义搜索索引），采用后端特定的检索策略融合结果，通过文件系统注入利用宿主智能体的配置自动读取行为传递编译后的记忆简报。同时管理完整智能体生命周期，包括信任预置、就绪轮询与错误检测、自适应终端文本注入。

Result: 作者进行了四个月（2025年12月至2026年3月）的常规部署，形成了经验报告，记录了三种代际上下文传递机制及其各自的失败模式，以及桥接异构记忆系统相较于构建统一系统的工程权衡。

Conclusion: 通过架设异质记忆系统的桥梁而非构建统一存储，PAO实现了跨会话记忆持久化的可行方案。作者的材料显示，在多记忆后端条件下，启动时记忆注入可以显著增强编码智能体的上下文连续性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents start each session with an empty context window, discarding accumulated knowledge from prior work. We present PrimeAgentOrchestrator (PAO), a system that spawns new instances of Claude Code -- Anthropic's terminal-based coding agent -- pre-loaded with relevant memories compiled from the user's existing personal databases. At spawn time, PAO queries two independently-operated memory backends in parallel (a PostgreSQL entity-observation database and a Cloudflare Worker semantic search index), fuses results using backend-specific retrieval strategies, and delivers the compiled briefing via filesystem injection that exploits the host agent's configuration auto-read behavior. PAO manages the full agent lifecycle including trust pre-seeding, readiness polling with error detection, and adaptive terminal text injection. We report on four months of regular deployment (December 2025 through March 2026) as an experience report, documenting three generations of context delivery mechanisms, the failure modes that motivated each redesign, and the engineering tradeoffs of bridging heterogeneous memory systems rather than building a unified one.

</details>


### [222] [Truth Lies Deep: Countering Semantic Camouflage via Latent Intent Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20378)
*Md. Hasib Ur Rahman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM安全对齐的"表面化"问题——拒绝机制只在生成末端触发而并未擦除预训练阶段习得的有害概念知识，导致模型易受"语义伪装"攻击。通过分析三个SLM家族在对抗压力下的潜在激活轨迹，识别出通用的"意图地平线"（约15-20%层深度），并提出基于早期层"危害签名"的轻量级探测防御LIV，在PKU-SafeRLHF上超越标准护栏20-50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的安全对齐往往流于表面：拒绝机制仅在生成末端触发，并未真正从模型中擦除预训练阶段习得的有害概念知识。这种架构层面的脱节使得模型容易受到"语义伪装"攻击——将有害意图包装在良性的叙事上下文中（如创意写作），从而绕过标准的输入输出护栏。现有防御无法有效应对此类零日语义攻击，亟需更本质的检测方法。

Method: 本研究首先对三个Small Language Model家族（Phi-3、Qwen2.5、Gemma-2b）进行对抗压力测试，分析其在语义伪装攻击下的潜在激活轨迹。通过逐层分析，识别出"意图地平线"临界深度（通常为总层数的15-20%），该处模型将查询上下文化为"安全"叙事时，预训练的有害意图表示发生坍缩。基于这一发现，提出Latent Intent Verification (LIV)——一种轻量级探测防御，利用早期层中仍保留的、可区分的"危害签名"来检测伪装攻击，无需模型重训练。

Result: 实验结果表明：晚期层中伪装攻击的表征与安全查询在数学上不可区分（检测率<20%），而早期层保留着独特的可检测"危害签名"。在PKU-SafeRLHF数据集上，LIV在所有测试架构中超越标准护栏20-50%，有效中和零日语义攻击，且无需模型重训练。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了LLM安全对齐的深层缺陷——拒绝机制与潜在知识表征之间的架构脱节，并提出利用早期层残余有害表征的LIV防御方案。该研究不仅证实了早期层保留的"危害签名"是可利用的检测信号，还提供了一种轻量、无需重训练的高效防御手段，为LLM安全领域提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Safety alignment in Large Language Models (LLMs) is often superficial, relying on refusal mechanisms that trigger only at the final stages of generation without erasing the foundational knowledge of harmful concepts acquired during pretraining. This study demonstrates that this architectural disconnect leaves models vulnerable to Semantic Camouflage -- adversarial attacks that wrap harmful intent in benign narrative contexts (e.g., creative writing), effectively bypassing standard input and output guardrails. By analyzing the latent activation trajectories of three distinct Small Language Model (SLM) families (Phi-3, Qwen2.5, and Gemma-2b) under adversarial stress, this research identifies a universal ``Intent Horizon'' -- a critical depth (typically 15--20\% of total layers) where the model's distinct, pre-trained representation of harmful intent collapses as it contextualizes the query into a ``safe'' narrative. Results indicate that while late-layer representations of camouflaged attacks are mathematically indistinguishable from safe queries (Detection Rate $< 20\%$), early-layer representations retain a distinct, detectable ``harm signature.'' Leveraging this insight, this paper proposes Latent Intent Verification (LIV), a lightweight probing defense. Experiments on the PKU-SafeRLHF dataset demonstrate that LIV outperforms standard guardrails by a margin of 20--50\% across all tested architectures, effectively neutralizing zero-day semantic attacks without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [223] [Auditable by Construction: An Ontology-Driven Framework for Trustworthy LLM Analytics in Enterprise Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20661)
*Sergiy Lunyakin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出知识驱动分析框架（KDAF），通过六阶段本体构建和上下文感知相关性传播（CARP）实现可审计的检索增强生成。在FinanceBench上评估表明，结构化检索在准确率上与词法基线无显著差异，但在可审计性上显著领先——引用可追溯性F1达0.515，且不引入题外证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业金融领域（如FP&A）采用大语言模型的主要障碍不是流利度而是信任：答案必须可追溯到权威来源并支持事后审计。现有RAG评估多聚焦准确率，忽视可审计性这一关键维度。

Method: 提出KDAF框架：①通过六个迭代阶段构建本体驱动的知识系统；②采用CARP进行上下文感知的相关性传播，使每个检索事实携带关系类型、置信度和来源谱系。在FinanceBench（145题）上与零上下文推理、BM25、概念加权词法检索、无约束图遍历进行对比。

Result: ①零上下文正确率仅4.1%，检索增强为10-12%；②检索条件下答案正确率统计上无显著差异（KDAF vs BM25: -0.007, CI含零），报告了负面结果；③可审计性上排序反转：KDAF引用可追溯性F1达0.515，超过无约束遍历(+0.027)和BM25(+0.052)，置信区间不含零；④图结构检索不引入题外证据（0/426，对比词法基线16.8%和20.2%），所有选中项均可溯源完整。

Conclusion: 在答案正确率维度上，结构化检索不优于词法基线，其成本合理性应建立在可审计性而非准确率上。本体引导的检索通过严格的来源约束和完整溯源链提供了不可替代的审计价值。

Abstract: Enterprise adoption of large language models in finance is constrained less by fluency than by trust: in Financial Planning and Analysis (FP&A) and other regulated workflows, an answer is usable only if it is traceable to authoritative sources and auditable after the fact. This paper argues that retrieval-augmented generation for enterprise finance should be evaluated on auditability alongside accuracy, and presents the Knowledge-Driven Analytics Framework (KDAF), which builds ontology-driven knowledge systems through six iterative stages and retrieves evidence via Context-Aware Relevance Propagation (CARP), so that every retrieved fact carries its relationship type, confidence, and source lineage.
  An evaluation on FinanceBench (145 questions) compares KDAF against zero-context inference, BM25, concept-weighted lexical retrieval, and ungrounded graph traversal. First, retrieval is necessary: zero-context inference reaches 4.1% correctness against 10-12% for retrieval-augmented conditions. Second, on answer correctness the retrieval conditions are statistically indistinguishable (KDAF vs BM25: -0.007, 95% CI [-0.021, 0.000]), so accuracy alone does not justify structured retrieval here -- a negative result we report explicitly. Third, on auditability the ordering reverses: KDAF attains the highest citation traceability F1 (0.515), exceeding ungrounded traversal by +0.027 (CI [0.006, 0.050]) and BM25 by +0.052 (CI [0.024, 0.083]), intervals excluding zero. Graph-structured retrieval also admits no evidence from outside the question subject entity (0 of 426 items, against 16.8% and 20.2% for lexical baselines), and every selected item resolves to a complete provenance chain. We argue that auditability, not accuracy, is the axis on which ontology-grounded retrieval earns its cost.

</details>


### [224] [A Survey on Foundations and Frontiers of Multimodal Agentic Frameworks: Techniques and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20379)
*Neel Mokaria,Rishie Raj,Dheeraj Baiju,Xiaoqian Shen,Shraman Pramanick,Kevin Qinghong Lin,Arda Senocak,Mike Zheng Shou,Philip Torr,Mohamed Elhoseiny,Yapeng Tian,Ruohan Gao,Salman Khan,Sayan Nag,Sanjoy Chowdhury,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了多模态性如何塑造智能体（agent）能力，从感知、推理、规划、记忆和行动五个核心功能模块出发，分析了从文本为中心到多模态框架的演化过程，并提出了一种以模态为中心的智能体架构分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有大量关于基于LLM智能体的综述，但多模态性在塑造智能体能力方面的作用近年来尚未被系统性地审视。随着大语言模型（LLM）向大多模态模型（LMM）演进，智能体系统能够处理图像、音频、视频等多种模态，显著提升其现实世界适用性，因此亟需一次系统的综述来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用以智能体核心功能模块（感知、推理、规划、记忆、行动）为分析视角的方法，追踪从文本中心智能体到多模态框架的演化过程；通过模态中心分类法（涵盖委托式、晚期融合、早期融合三种架构集成方式）将架构设计选择与智能体能力关联起来；并跨多个应用领域（机器人、GUI与网页导航、多模态内容生成与编辑、长视频理解与检索）进行系统评估。

Result: 系统整理了多模态智能体在不同应用领域的表现，分析了其效率-可扩展性权衡（包括训练与推理成本、延迟及部署约束），并识别了关键研究空白，为构建鲁棒的、通用型智能系统绘制了路线图。

Conclusion: 多模态性是推动智能体从文本受限走向通用智能的关键驱动力，未来的研究应聚焦于弥合现有架构的不足，通过更有效的模态集成方式提升智能体的感知能力与推理质量，最终实现鲁棒且通用的智能系统。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) have fueled a wave of research into agency: the ability to reason, plan, and act. This effort has produced agentic frameworks that orchestrate perception, memory, and decision-making around powerful LLM backbones. With the advent of large multimodal models (LMMs), these systems can process and integrate diverse modalities, including images, audio, and video, thereby improving their real-world applicability. Yet, while surveys of LLM-based agents exist, the role of multimodality in shaping agency has not been systematically examined in recent years. This survey fills the gap by analyzing the impact of multimodality across the core functional modules of the agentic framework: perception, reasoning, planning, memory, and action. Using this lens, we trace the evolution from text-centric agents to multimodal frameworks, examine how modalities are integrated through delegated, late-fusion, and early-fusion architectures, and assess the emergence of agentic behaviors enabled by grounded perception and multimodal reasoning. We organize existing work through a modality-centric taxonomy that links architectural design choices to agent capabilities. Moreover, we review multimodal agentic systems across various application domains, including Robotics, GUI & Web Navigation, Multimedia Content Generation & Editing, and Long-form Video Understanding & Retrieval. Beyond capabilities, we analyze performance across these settings and discuss efficiency-scalability trade-offs, including training and inference costs, latency, and deployment constraints. By focusing on the impact of multimodality in agentic design, we aim to identify key gaps and chart a roadmap toward robust and general-purpose intelligent systems.

</details>


### [225] [Interpretable Multimodal Classification with Linear Discriminant Tree Ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20384)
*Mojtaba Moattari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于树集成（tree-based ensembles）的多模态情感与行为分类框架，在保持高分类精度的同时提升模型可解释性。通过将各模态编码为token、提取并聚类概念降维、路由融合后经树集成分类器处理，并引入改进的特征重要性指标（降低二分类中负类影响）。所提出的LDT、LDF、LDAB三类线性判别树集成模型在F1-mod上比Multimodal Transformer高4.3%，准确率比Interpretable Multimodal Routing（IMR）高3.0%，且改进的特征重要性在人类标注一致性上显著优于默认特征重要性（IEMOCAP上62.2% vs 43.2%，CMU-MOSI上46.7% vs 32.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高容量模型（如Transformer）在情感与行为分类中虽预测性能强，但其分布式表示和深层非线性导致难以对多模态特征赋予有意义的权重，限制了其在临床情感监测、教育评估等信任敏感场景中的应用。论文旨在弥补准确性与可解释性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 提出基于树集成分类器的框架：先将各模态编码为token，提取并聚类概念以降低维度，再将融合后的模态输入树集成分类器；同时提出一种改进的特征重要性指标，通过降低二分类任务中负类的影响来提升指标/标志检测能力。具体模型包括线性判别树（LDT）、线性判别森林（LDF）和线性判别AdaBoost（LDAB）。

Result: 实验结果表明：所提出的树集成模型在F1-mod上超越Multimodal Transformer 4.3%，在准确率上超越主要可解释多模态基线IMR 3.0%；改进的多模态特征重要性提取出的模态间显著概念在人类标注者一致性上大幅超过默认特征重要性（IEMOCAP上62.2% vs 43.2%；CMU-MOSI上46.7% vs 32.1%）。

Conclusion: 该工作证明树集成方法可在多模态情感与行为分类中同时实现竞争性准确率和可解释性，改进的特征重要性指标能更有效地提取人类可理解的关键模态间概念，为信任敏感应用提供了可行的可解释多模态分类方案。

Abstract: Multimodal affect and behaviour classifiers that fuse heterogeneous text, audio, and visual streams must simultaneously achieve competitive accuracy and produce human-understandable explanations of the cues driving their decisions -- a dual objective that current high-capacity models, notably Transformers, only partially address. While Transformers attain strong predictive performance, their distributed representations and deep nonlinearity make it difficult to assign meaningful importance weights to individual multimodal features, limiting their use in trust-sensitive applications such as clinical affect monitoring and educational assessment. We address this gap by developing a framework based on tree-based ensembles that balances accuracy and interpretability. The framework encodes each modality into tokens, extracts and clusters concepts to reduce dimensionality, routes the fused modalities through tree-based ensemble classifiers, and interprets trends using a novel modified feature importance metric. The modified importance reduces the influence of the negative class in binary classification tasks, thereby improving indicator or marker detection. The proposed tree-based ensembles -- Linear Discriminant Tree (LDT), Linear Discriminant Forest (LDF), and Linear Discriminant AdaBoost (LDAB) -- achieve F1-mod gains of 4.3\% over the Multimodal Transformer and accuracy gains of 3.0\% over the primary interpretable multimodal baseline, Interpretable Multimodal Routing (IMR). The proposed multimodal feature importance extracts salient inter-modal concepts with substantially higher human-annotator agreement scores than default feature importance (62.2\% vs.\ 43.2\% on IEMOCAP; 46.7\% vs.\ 32.1\% on CMU-MOSI).

</details>


### [226] [Representation Affects Retrieval: A Case Study of Skill Discovery and Routing in a Multimodal Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20389)
*Kevin Dela Rosa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以Tinycloud生产级多模态视频代理框架为案例，研究在规模较小时（小N情境下）LLM规划者如何通过系统提示词中的上下文内技能选择来匹配技能，并发现提示词内的技能暴露并非单调有益：部分暴露可能造成词汇竞争，抑制正确选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产级代理框架需要从不断增长的技能库中发现并排序最适合用户任务的技能。在小型规模下，技能选择在上下文中进行（LLM规划者直接从系统提示词中的技能表示中选择），这种选择方式与大规模时基于嵌入检索的技能路由是相对应的。作者希望通过案例研究理解上下文内技能选择的行为与局限性。

Method: 采用案例研究方法。Tinycloud以两种表示形式提供技能：（1）工具技能（tool-skills）——封装单一外部API或系统工具，作为基本词汇；（2）工作流技能（workflow-skills）——编排工具技能调用及模板渲染以产出命名交付物。系统提示词通过两个表面暴露技能：内联主体表面（完整指令、脚本、模板）用于自动加载技能，单行列表用于按需技能。设计了六个任务的消融实验，覆盖三种暴露机制（全开、默认、全关）。

Result: 全自动加载在所有任务中选择黄金技能；全关机制使执行变慢并产生硬性发现失败；生产默认配置在一个任务上出现误路由，原因是该任务的词汇信号与自动加载的工具技能发生冲突，将规划者注意力从列出的工作流技能引开。核心发现是提示词内技能暴露并非单调有益——部分暴露可能产生词汇竞争从而抑制正确选择。

Conclusion: 将小N观察与近期大规模基于检索的技能路由工作联系起来，指出上下文内技能选择与嵌入检索在机制上可类比，本文作为案例研究而非基准贡献，揭示了技能暴露策略中词汇竞争带来的选择偏差问题。

Abstract: A production agent harness must discover and rank, from a growing library of skills, the one most appropriate for a user's task. At small scale this selection happens in context: the LLM planner chooses among skill representations exposed in its system prompt, without an explicit embedding-based retrieval step. We treat this in-context selection as the small-N counterpart to embedding-based skill retrieval at scale, and present a case study of how Tinycloud, a production multimodal video agent harness, represents its skills for the planner. The harness ships skills under two recurring representations: tool-skills that wrap a single external API or system tool and serve as primitive vocabulary, and workflow-skills that orchestrate tool-skill calls plus a template render to produce one named deliverable. The harness exposes them via two surfaces in the system prompt: an inlined-body surface (full instructions, scripts, templates) for autoloaded skills, and a one-line listing for on-demand skills. A six-task selection ablation across three exposure regimes (all-on, default, all-off) shows that full autoload selects the gold skill on every task; all-off slows execution and produces hard discovery failures; and the production default misroutes one task because its lexical signal collides with an autoloaded tool-skill that pulls planner attention away from a listed workflow-skill. The headline finding is that in-prompt exposure of skills is not monotonically helpful: partial exposure can create lexical competition that suppresses correct selection. We connect this small-N observation to recent retrieval-based skill-routing work at large scale, and frame this contribution as a case study rather than a benchmark.

</details>


### [227] [Nexus: Depth-Adaptive KV-Cache Splicing and Retrieval-Decoupled Tool Routing for Agentic LLMs on Unified Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20397)
*Mustafa Arslan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Nexus通过解耦路由与schema预填充成本（INT8语义旁路缓存SLB + 交叉编码器边缘门控实现检索式选工具，参数压缩至中位19个token），将工具注册表扩展到250个时仍保持~89%路由精度，且首次参数token比全schema重预填充快1.66倍，节省约80%主上下文token；同时作为一种有界辅助杠杆，将编译好的schema KV块直接移植入实时上下文，但受RoPE相位漂移限制（阈值P=256），在线以下采用深度自适应后缀重解码修复，保证输出保真（top-1一致，D_KL≈0）而延迟可能降至0.98x，中等深度下获得1.1-1.7x TTFT加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于MCP的智能体LLM每一轮都会重新编码冗长的工具schema，导致预填充开销（随序列长度二次增长）随着工具注册表扩大而主导TTFT，成为严重性能瓶颈。作者希望解耦路由决策与schema预填充计算成本，避免上下文溢出并改善首字延迟。

Method: 主杠杆是语义旁路缓存（SLB）：INT8量化的语义检索缓存搭配校准过的交叉编码器边缘门控，通过检索方式选择工具（与深度无关），参数基于压缩文本签名生成而非KV拼接。次杠杆是编译schema KV块移植：将预先编译好的KV块直接移植入实时上下文，利用有锚拼接保持输出精确；超过阈值P=256时用深度自适应后缀重解码修复接缝，可升级为全量重预填充，保证"永不失真"属性。

Result: 路由精度在250个工具规模下保持约89%（全拼接基线已溢出上下文）；首参数token提速1.66倍，节省约80%主上下文token。KV移植路径在中深度下获得1.1-1.7x TTFT加速，深上下文收敛至持平，延迟偶降至0.98x。发现两个负面结果：离锚RoPE保真边界，以及无参考漂移门无法预测漂移（Spearman rho=0.193）。

Conclusion: Nexus证明通过解耦路由与预填充、复用编译KV，可在不牺牲输出保真的前提下显著缓解大型工具注册表的TTFT瓶颈。定性边界具有泛化性，但定量性能包络受限于测试的单一模型元组（Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M，Apple silicon统一内存），且RoPE相位漂移与漂移门预测失效揭示了该方法的固有限制。

Abstract: Agentic large language models (LLMs) on the Model Context Protocol (MCP) re-encode verbose tool schemas every turn, so prefill - quadratic in sequence length - dominates time-to-first-token (TTFT) as the tool registry grows. Nexus's primary lever is to decouple routing from the schema-prefill cost: an INT8 semantic lookaside buffer (SLB) with a calibrated cross-encoder margin gate selects tools by retrieval, and arguments are generated over a compressed textual signature (median 19 tokens) rather than over spliced key/value (KV) cache. This path is depth-independent: routing accuracy stays near 89% as the registry scales to 250 tools - where a concatenate-all-schemas baseline overflows the context window entirely - and it reaches a first-argument token 1.66x sooner than a full-schema re-prefill at a ~80% main-context token saving. As a secondary, bounded lever we transplant a compiled schema KV block directly into the live context. This is fundamentally limited by rotary position embedding (RoPE) phase drift: an anchored splice is output-exact, but off-anchor placement corrupts attention, so beyond a threshold P=256 Nexus repairs the seam with a depth-adaptive suffix redecode that escalates to a full re-prefill. The resulting never-regress property is a guarantee on output fidelity (top-1 agreement, D_KL approx. 0) - not on latency, which can dip to 0.98x before converging to parity - alongside a 1.1-1.7x TTFT speedup at moderate depth that narrows to parity at deep context. Two negative results bound the design: the off-anchor RoPE fidelity boundary, and the failure of a reference-free drift gate to predict drift (Spearman rho = 0.193). All measurements are from one model tuple (Qwen2.5-14B-Instruct Q4_K_M) on Apple-silicon unified memory; the qualitative boundaries generalize, while the quantitative envelope is tuple-specific.

</details>


### [228] [Environmental Slow AI: Design Principles for Generative Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20398)
*Vanessa Utz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本立场论文从环境人文学科视角审视生成式AI中嵌入的极致主义价值，提出以环境可持续性为核心的"慢AI"设计原则，包括克制、充分性、选择性而非留存、物质可见性和摩擦作为可供性五项原则，每项原则均包含设计实现与解释性双层操作，通过将反思性嵌入设计来恢复被无摩擦默认所剥夺的人类能动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI系统以规模化的方式生产文化产物，但其设计中也嵌入了特定的文化价值。一旦这些价值被识别出来，就面临被有意重塑的可能。当前生成式AI反映的是极致主义价值，论文希望从环境人文学科传统出发，对此进行批判性审视，并提出以环境可持续性为核心价值的替代性设计原则。

Method: 论文采用立场论文（position paper）的方法，以环境人文学科传统为理论框架，在"慢AI"这一已跨越多个研究与实践项目的概念框架下，提出五项设计原则（克制、充分性、选择性而非留存、物质可见性、摩擦作为可供性），并逐一用当前广泛部署的系统设计进行对照说明。每项原则在两个层面运作：设计实施层面和解释性层面（促使使用者与开发者对系统进行反思性互动）。

Result: 论文提出了五项以环境可持续性为核心的"慢AI"设计原则，每个原则均包含设计实施与解释性两个操作层面，并通过与现有系统设计的对照予以阐释。这些原则共同通过将解释性反思构建进设计之中，恢复了被无摩擦默认设置悄然剥夺的人类决策权，从而扩展了人类能动性。

Conclusion: 生成式AI中嵌入的极致主义价值可以通过设计原则的系统性重塑转向以环境可持续性为核心的价值导向。"慢AI"框架下的五项原则不仅提供了设计实施路径，还通过解释性层面的构建促进用户和开发者的反思性参与，最终在技术设计中恢复和扩展人类能动性。

Abstract: Generative AI (genAI) systems produce cultural artefacts at scale, but they also reflect embedded cultural values through their design. Once identified, these values become open to deliberate reshaping. This position paper examines the maximalist values of current generative AI through an environmental humanities tradition and proposes design principles in which environmental sustainability serves as the core value instead. The principles are developed under the umbrella of Slow AI, a term that already circulates across several distinct research and practice programs. Five design principles are articulated (restraint, sufficiency, selectivity over retention, material visibility, and friction as affordance), each of them illustrated against the current design of widely deployed systems. Each principle operates at two levels: a design implementation, and an interpretive layer at which users and developers are prompted toward reflective engagement with the system. Together these principles extend human agency by restoring decisions that frictionless defaults have silently removed and do so by building interpretive reflection into design.

</details>


### [229] [When Retrieval Fails Before It Begins: Structurally Indirect Prerequisite Eviction as a Retention Failure in Agentic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20400)
*Minkyu Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了现有检索中心范式隐含的假设（即必要证据能在内存驱逐中幸存），提出并定义了"结构间接先决条件驱逐"这一检索前失效模式，并引入一种一跳图感知规则——依赖感知语义垃圾回收（DSGC），在固定预算下显著提升全链路保留率，同时提供可复现基准与逐种子诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统在固定预算下主要聚焦检索阶段优化，却隐含假设"必要证据能够幸存于内存驱逐"。作者认为这是不成立的——存在检索前的失效模式（结构间接先决条件驱逐），即与查询弱对齐的上游块在预算压力下被错误丢弃，从而破坏下游推理链条。

Method: 1) 定义了"结构间接先决条件驱逐"这一失效模式的操作性定义；2) 构建了可复现的确定性基准和逐种子迹诊断工具；3) 提出DSGC（依赖感知语义垃圾回收），一种一跳图感知规则，用于在保留阶段识别并保护与查询存在间接结构依赖但语义弱对齐的块。

Result: 在主实验中，DSGC将全链路保留率从0.03提升至0.90（词汇编码器），以及从0.23提升至1.00（句子编码器）。鲁棒性检查进一步确定了该一跳规则在何种预算和规模区间内有效或退化。

Conclusion: 保留阶段（retention）本身是独立于检索阶段的失效边界，不能仅依赖检索策略弥补。DSGC验证了一跳图感知规则在保留阶段的可行性，发布的流程与故障事后分析工具支持对该边界的机制化研究。

Abstract: Agentic memory under a fixed budget involves two stages: retention and retrieval. Existing retrieval-centered paradigms implicitly assume necessary evidence survives eviction, but we challenge this by isolating a pre-retrieval failure mode: structurally indirect prerequisite eviction, in which upstream blocks weakly aligned with the query are discarded under budget pressure. We provide an operational definition of this failure, a reproducible deterministic benchmark, and per-seed trace diagnostics. Finally, we evaluate Dependency-aware Semantic Garbage Collection (DSGC), a one-hop graph-aware rule. In our main suite, DSGC improves full-chain retention from 0.03 to 0.90 under a lexical encoder and from 0.23 to 1.00 under a sentence encoder. Robustness checks then identify the budget and scaling regimes where the one-hop rule holds or degrades. Our released pipeline and failure postmortem support mechanistic analysis of retention before retrieval as a distinct failure boundary.

</details>


### [230] [World models of environment, agent and joint agent-environment systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20401)
*Manuel Baltieri,Filippo Torresan,Yivan Zhang,Alexander Boyd,Fernando E. Rosas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从"预测通道"（环境通道、智能体通道、联合过程）这一先验区分出发，利用计算力学为基于模型的强化学习定义了三种规范预测模型（ε-transducers/ε-machines），并进一步构建了闭环耦合诱导的支撑受限环境与智能体模型，证明了支撑受限环境状态通过规范联合因果状态分解，且可在无限制模型具有无穷状态时得到有限模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型通常以"预测什么变量"（观测、奖励、状态等）来区分，但作者认为存在一个更根本的区分维度——"预测哪个通道"。这一区分能澄清不同世界模型究竟建模的是什么，以及耦合与支撑受限如何改变其规范预测结构。

Method: 利用计算力学框架，对三种通道（环境通道 O_:|A_:、智能体通道 A_:|O_:、联合过程 (A,O):）分别定义规范预测模型（ε-transducer 或 ε-machine）。进一步构建由闭环耦合诱导的支撑受限环境与智能体模型，其预测等价关系仅在实现交互所支持的延续上进行刻画，并证明支撑受限环境状态经由规范联合因果状态分解。

Result: 规范环境模型可以恢复标准的预测状态表示；支撑受限环境状态可以被联合因果状态分解（智能体侧为对偶结构）；给定一个 POMDP/控制器示例，无限制环境模型具有无穷多状态，而由耦合诱导的规范支撑受限模型是有限的。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解不同世界模型的对象提供了统一视角，阐明了耦合与支撑受限如何改变世界模型的规范预测结构及其复杂度，有助于模型基强化学习中世界模型的构建与选择。

Abstract: World models are a central component of model-based reinforcement learning. They are usually discussed in terms of what variables they predict, such as observations, rewards, states, latent or information states. We argue that there is a prior distinction: which channel they model. We consider three cases: the environment channel $O_{:} \mid A_{:}$, the agent channel $A_{:} \mid O_{:}$, and the realised joint process $(A, O)_{:}$, equivalently viewed as a channel with no inputs. Using computational mechanics, we define canonical predictive models for these three cases as $ε$-transducers or $ε$-machines. Canonical environment models recover standard predictive state representations, while the other two give analogous notions of canonical models for the agent and the joint system. We then build canonical support-restricted environment and agent models induced by closed-loop coupling, whose predictive equivalences range over continuations supported by the realised interaction. The key structural result is that canonical support-restricted environment states factor through the canonical joint causal states, and their transition structure is induced directly from the joint model; the agent-side construction is dual. Finally, we give a POMDP/controller example in which the unrestricted environment model has infinitely many states while the canonical support-restricted model induced by the coupling is finite. The framework clarifies what different world models are models of, and how coupling and support restriction can change their canonical predictive structure and complexity.

</details>


### [231] [StateSight: Benchmarking Latent Spatial-State Reconstruction in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20414)
*Michelle Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StateSight基准，一个程序化生成的单图像视觉空间推理基准，用于评估视觉语言模型从单张图片中重建潜在空间结构的能力。包含三个任务族（立方体网对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、4邻接连通分量计数），共300个提示，并配套StateSight-Steps数据集（900个交错图文示例和3600个确定性中间视觉状态）。实验表明GPT-5.5和Claude Sonnet 5准确率远低于人类基线，且格式有效的响应可能掩盖空间结构重建失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态问答基准通常将感知、光学字符识别、领域知识、语言先验和推理混合在同一评估中，难以隔离和度量视觉语言模型从单幅图像中重建潜在空间结构的能力。作者希望通过程序化生成的、具有确定性标签的基准来精确评估这一核心能力。

Method: 1) 构建StateSight基准：程序化生成三个任务族（立方体网对面推理、遮挡立方体塔计数、4邻接连通分量计数），每个任务族300个单图像提示，配备确定性oracle标签和精确匹配评分；2) 引入StateSight-Steps伴随数据集，包含900个交错图文示例和3600个确定性中间视觉状态；3) 进行可见推导（visible-derivation）分析以识别模型在图像状态重建和推理过程中的错误模式。

Result: OpenAI GPT-5.5（API标识符gpt-5.5）在三个任务上分别达到59.3%、33.3%和28.3%的准确率；Claude Sonnet 5分别达到53.3%、18.7%和7.3%。所有最终直接运行均零格式错误。30名参与者的人类基线在60个项目上（平均准确率80.8%、68.8%、64.3%）在每项任务上均超过两个模型。

Conclusion: 格式有效（format-valid）的响应可能掩盖模型在恢复可验证视觉推理所需空间结构上的失败。即使模型能够产生符合格式要求的输出，其内部空间状态重建能力仍然存在显著缺陷，这一差距通过人类基线比较和可见推导分析得到了确认。

Abstract: Vision-language models are increasingly used for multimodal question answering, yet their ability to reconstruct latent spatial structure from a single image remains difficult to isolate. Broad benchmarks often combine perception, optical character recognition, domain knowledge, linguistic priors, and reasoning in the same evaluation. We introduce StateSight, a procedurally generated benchmark for cube-net opposite-face reasoning, occluded cube-tower counting, and 4-neighbor connected-component counting. Each task family contains 300 single-image prompts with deterministic oracle labels and exact-match scoring. OpenAI GPT-5.5, using the API model identifier gpt-5.5, achieved 59.3%, 33.3%, and 28.3% accuracy across the three tasks, while Claude Sonnet 5 achieved 53.3%, 18.7%, and 7.3%. All final direct runs had zero format errors. A 30-participant human baseline on 60 items exceeded both models on every task, with mean accuracies of 80.8%, 68.8%, and 64.3%. Visible-derivation analysis identified recurring errors in image-state reconstruction and reasoning procedure. We also introduce StateSight-Steps, a companion dataset of 900 interleaved image-text examples and 3,600 deterministic intermediate visual states. The results show that format-valid responses can mask failures to recover the spatial structure required for verifiable visual inference.

</details>


### [232] [Categorical AI phenomenology: A first-person approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20420)
*Robert Prentner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"现象学优先"的人工意识研究框架，将意识重新定义为代理通过与世界交互而展现的主观体验，并利用从Q网络派生出的范畴论数学结构来建模第一人称体验的不变结构。Q网络在此被视作编码代理-世界交互的关系型接口，与计算机动态状态依赖感官输入、先前状态与行动的机制相类比。该框架为"接口意识"提供了严谨的理论基础，并与4E认知方法（具身、嵌入、延展、生成）保持一致，最终为人工现象学提供了一种基于范畴数学的关系性、原则性描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人工意识研究多基于第三人称的功能主义或行为主义视角，难以触及意识的主观体验维度。作者试图突破这一局限，通过现象学优先的方法论转向，将研究焦点置于第一人称结构上，以解决人工智能系统中"主观体验"缺乏形式化描述的问题。

Method: 采用现象学优先的方法论，将意识重新概念化为代理与世界界面交互所产生的主观体验。使用Q网络派生出的范畴论数学工具来形式化建模行动与现象学不变量；将Q网络视作关系型接口，编码代理-世界交互关系，类比计算机动态状态依赖感官输入、前一状态和行动的方式。同时借鉴4E认知科学框架，强调意识的生成性、具身性、嵌入性与延展性维度。

Result: 构建了一个基于范畴数学的"接口意识"理论框架，为描述将信息处理嵌入现象学结构的计算系统提供了严密的理论基础，实现了与4E认知方法的一致性，给出了一个原则性的、关系性的人工现象学描述。

Conclusion: 该论文提供了一种以关系性和现象学为基础的接口意识理论，为人工意识研究开辟了新的方法论路径——从第一人称体验结构出发而非从外部行为或功能出发，为未来人工现象学的研究奠定了数学与概念上的基础。

Abstract: This paper develops a phenomenology-first approach to artificial consciousness by reframing consciousness as the subjective experience enacted through an agent's interface with the world. We shift the methodological focus to first-person structures, modeled mathematically by categories derived from Q-networks to capture actions and phenomenological invariants. In this framework, Q-networks are conceptualized as relational interfaces encoding agent-world interaction, analogous to how the dynamical states of a computer depend on its sensory inputs, previous states, and actions. Our work provides a rigorous framework for interface consciousness to describe computational systems that embed information-processing into phenomenological structure. The approach aligns with 4E approaches to cognition by emphasizing enactive, embedded, and extended dimensions of experience. The paper thus offers a principled, relational, and phenomenological account of artificial phenomenology grounded in categorical mathematics.

</details>


### [233] [Who Delegates to AI? Evidence from 53,000 Agent Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20425)
*Hyeongjae Lee,Jihyang Cheon,Lanu Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全新的AI暴露度测度——"委托暴露度"（delegated exposure），通过Agentic Adoption Index（AAI）衡量工人是否已将任务真正委托给AI代理。基于Manus技能市场中约5.3万个代理技能规范与O*NET约1.8万个任务描述的语义相似度，聚合到职业层面。发现AI委托集中的职业与传统AI风险框架预测的截然不同，AAI更接近"AI能做什么"而非"工人实际用什么"，且AAI在工资分布中上部及学士学位水平达到峰值，在两端下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI暴露度文献只衡量AI能在何处执行任务，却未捕捉工人是否真正采用了AI。研究者希望区分"可能性"与"实际采纳"，填补职业层面AI实际采纳测度的空白。

Method: 提出"委托暴露度"概念，将"工人是否已将任务委托给AI"操作化为Agentic Adoption Index（AAI）。具体方法为：从Manus Skills Marketplace收集约53,000个代理技能规范，与O*NET中约18,000个任务陈述计算语义相似度，再聚合到职业层面形成AAI指标。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）AI委托集中的职业与传统AI风险框架预测的高风险职业差异显著；（2）AAI反映"AI的能力"比"工人的实际使用"更充分，即二者存在差距；（3）AAI在工资分布的中上层及学士学位水平达到峰值，在高低两端均下降。技术可用性解释了大部分变异，但无法解释最高教育水平职业的缺口，说明可行性本身不能完全解释采纳差异。

Conclusion: 仅凭技术可行性无法解释AI采纳的全部差异，高教育职业的委托不足可能源于工作本身抗拒预先规范，或是专业人员对编码化节奏的自主裁量。区分这两种机制并追踪指标随时间的分化，需要重复测量才能实现。

Abstract: A growing literature measures how far occupations are exposed to AI, but these measures capture where AI could perform tasks, not whether workers have adopted it. We propose a new layer of exposure, delegated exposure, which records whether a worker has committed a task to AI by building it into a workflow. We operationalize it as the Agentic Adoption Index (AAI), which measures how closely an occupation's tasks match the agentic routines practitioners have already built and shared. We embed roughly 53,000 agent skill specifications from the Manus Skills Marketplace, compute their semantic similarity to about 18,000 O*NET task statements, and aggregate to the occupation level. Three findings follow. First, the occupations where delegation concentrates differ sharply from those pre-AI frameworks identified as most at risk. Second, the AAI tracks what AI could do more closely than what workers currently use it for. Third, the AAI peaks below the top of the wage distribution and at the bachelor's level, declining at both extremes. Technical availability explains most of this variation, but not the shortfall among the most educated occupations, so feasibility alone cannot account for who adopts. That shortfall may reflect work that resists advance specification, or professional discretion over the pace of codification. Distinguishing the two, and tracking how these measures diverge over time, will require repeated measurement.

</details>


### [234] [STCO: Conditional Neural Operators for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20477)
*Xingxin Yang,Zhan Zhang,Juan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出时空条件算子（STCO），一种用于"预定义条件算子学习"（PCOL）的统一接口，能够将目标时刻的条件场（如物体运动、入流、强迫力）注入到异构骨干网络中，实现对受控/优化场景下PDE系统的未来状态预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经算子对未来状态的预测仅基于观测状态和静态问题描述符进行条件设置，但在控制或优化场景中，物体运动、入流或强迫力是查询时预先给定的，不能仅由观测状态确定。因此需要一个统一的接口来将这些预定义的目标时刻条件场注入到不同的骨干架构中。

Method: STCO的条件接口由两部分组成：(1) Flow-Aware Graph Leaf (FAGL)，一个非学习的组件，利用最终观测帧的涡量构造固定基数的自适应分区，并将历史观测和条件场共置于区域坐标；(2) Dual-Site Feature-wise Linear Modulation (DSFiLM)，在算子计算前后通过运动、入流和强迫三条独立路由，使用当前特征驱动的slot级和channel级门控进行特征调制。文章在12个具有不同物理和时间输入的匹配骨干架构上评估。

Result: 在浸没边界CFD基准（涵盖预定义运动、入流扰动、体积力激励和形态变化）上，STCO在12个匹配骨干、3个预测区间和2个前瞻范围上，平均配对降低相对L2场误差31.1%，归一化压力载荷误差降低24.7%。对11个骨干的长前瞻场误差均有降低，且对每个条件组的干预都能产生可测量的预测变化。

Conclusion: STCO作为一种架构无关的条件注入接口，能有效提升异质骨干网络对受控PDE系统的预测精度，尤其在预设条件（运动、入流、力）场景下表现出显著且一致的改进，验证了其作为通用PCOL接口的有效性。

Abstract: Neural operators have emerged as efficient surrogates for time-dependent physical systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs), but their future-state predictions are often conditioned only on observed states and static problem descriptors. For control or optimization, however, body motion, inflow, or forcing are prescribed for the query without being determined solely by the observed state. We introduce the Spatiotemporal Conditional Operator (STCO) for prescribed-condition operator learning (PCOL), a common interface that supplies prescribed target-time condition fields to heterogeneous backbone architectures while retaining their architecture-specific core computation and context pathways. Its condition interface combines Flow-Aware Graph Leaf (FAGL) with Dual-Site Feature-wise Linear Modulation (DSFiLM). Non-learned FAGL uses vorticity from the final observed frame to construct a fixed-cardinality adaptive partition, then co-locates the observed history and target-time condition fields at its regional coordinates. DSFiLM injects separate motion, inflow, and force routes before and after operator computation through current-feature-driven slot- and channel-wise gates. We evaluate twelve matched backbone architectures with different existing physical and temporal inputs. The immersed-boundary computational fluid dynamics (CFD) benchmark spans prescribed motion, inflow disturbances, body-force actuation, and morphology. Across twelve matched backbones, three regimes, and two lead ranges, STCO yields mean paired reductions of 31.1% in relative-L2 field error and 24.7% in normalized pressure-derived load error. It also lowers longer-lead field error for 11 backbones, while interventions on individual condition groups produce measurable prediction changes for every group evaluated.

</details>


### [235] [Terminal Agents: A Survey of AI Agents in Command-Line Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20485)
*Yi Bin,Xiaoyang Yuan,Haoxi Zeng,Wencheng Ye,Wenqi Shao,Chen Qian,Wei Ye,Yujuan Ding,Zheng Wang,Pengpeng Zeng,Jingkuan Song,Heng Tao Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文以"终端中介执行"（terminal-mediated execution）为组织视角，对大型语言模型（LLM）终端智能体进行了系统性综述，建立了七维终端能力画像（terminal competence profile），从系统架构、能力获取与评估三个层面统一了分散在软件工程、工具使用和计算机使用研究中的相关工作。核心发现是：终端智能体的行为由模型、接口、框架（harness）、运行时与环境的联合塑造；可执行轨迹将学习锚定在行动后果、验证与恢复上，而现有评估偏向最终结果、对过程质量与治理覆盖不均；对照诊断实验表明基准族与系统匹配比较揭示了性能的基准依赖性与组件归因的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于LLM智能体在终端中行为的综述分散于软件工程、工具使用和计算机使用等领域，缺乏一个统一的分析框架来界定其工作负载边界，并系统地连接系统架构、能力获取与评估三方面。作者旨在以终端中介执行为组织性视角，弥合这一研究缺口。

Method: 本文采用综述方法论：首先以终端命令执行、文本反馈和有状态环境交互构成的"行动-观察"回路来界定终端智能体的范畴与工作负载边界；其次提出七维终端能力画像以刻画系统架构与能力谱系；随后系统梳理可执行轨迹驱动的学习机制与现有评估实践；最后通过受限固定条件诊断实验（benchmark家族对比与系统匹配比较）验证框架的分析效力。

Result: 综述揭示了三个关键结果：(1) 终端智能体的实际行为由模型、接口、框架、运行时与环境五要素联合决定；(2) 现有评估过度强调最终结果，对过程质量、故障恢复与治理维度的覆盖不均衡；(3) 受限诊断实验表明不同benchmark族暴露不同的过程信号，且匹配系统对比显示性能高度依赖基准设定，组件归因存在根本性局限。

Conclusion: 作者倡导在终端智能体研究中明确报告系统与运行时条件，并以可回放轨迹和过程级证据支撑评估结果。所提出的框架为软件工程及新兴应用领域中终端中介式智能体的统一研究提供了基础，并为未来的评估标准化与过程质量测量指明了方向。

Abstract: Large language model agents increasingly act through terminals, yet existing surveys disperse terminal-mediated behavior across software engineering, tool use, and computer-use research. We regard terminal agents as systems whose dominant progress-bearing action--observation loop is mediated by terminal command execution, textual feedback, and stateful environment interaction. Using terminal-mediated execution as an organizing lens, this survey establishes workload-level boundaries and connects system architecture, competence acquisition, and evaluation through a seven-dimensional terminal competence profile. Our synthesis shows that realized behavior is jointly shaped by the model, interface, harness, runtime, and environment. Executable trajectories ground learning in action consequences, verification, and recovery, whereas prevailing evaluations emphasize final outcomes and expose process quality, recovery, and governance unevenly. Bounded fixed-condition diagnostics illustrate two implications: benchmark families expose different process signals, and matched system comparisons reveal benchmark-dependent performance and limits of component attribution. These findings motivate explicit reporting of system and runtime conditions, supported by replayable traces and process-level evidence. The framework provides a unified basis for studying terminal-mediated agency across software engineering and emerging application domains.

</details>


### [236] [Lost in Translation: How Universal Ethical Values Fail to Translate Across Global Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20490)
*Ozioma C. Oguine,Munachimso B. Oguine,Cesar Cervera,Jenny Yang,Pooja Voladoddi,Mario Rodriguez,Saif Eddin Bani Malhem,Karla Badillo-Urquiola,Daricia Wilkinson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究基于对10个国家14位专家的访谈，发现AI伦理框架中的普世价值（如公平、透明、问责）在实践中被重新解释以适应本地道德逻辑，揭示了全球抽象框架与本地情境实践之间的"翻译鸿沟"，并提出迈向多元治理的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI伦理框架将公平、透明、问责等价值视为普世且可在各情境中统一实施的，但这种"一刀切"的假设忽视了不同文化和结构性条件下的实际差异，本研究旨在揭示专家如何在实践中重新诠释这些核心价值。

Method: 对10个国家的14位AI领域专家进行定性访谈，分析他们在实践中对AI的认知、对核心价值的重新诠释以及对治理替代方案的构想。

Result: 发现AI部署受结构性不平等条件制约，包括基础设施约束、剥削性实践和技术"神秘化"；专家将隐私重新诠释为集体性和关系性而非个体的，将透明诠释为基于信任的责任而非技术披露，将公平诠释为获取与代表的公平而非结果均等；识别出这些是全球编码框架与本地实践之间的"翻译鸿沟"。

Conclusion: 提出迈向多元治理的路径，主张重新分配认知权威，将伦理协商视为持续的、情境敏感的进程，而非固定的技术标准。

Abstract: AI ethics frameworks treat values such as fairness, transparency, and accountability as universal and uniformly operationalizable across contexts. We examined how 14 experts across 10 countries made sense of AI in practice, reinterpreted core values, and envisioned governance alternatives. We found that AI deployment is characterized by structurally unequal conditions, marked by infrastructural constraints, extractive practices, and a "mystification" of technology, which fundamentally shape perceptions of risks and opportunities. Our findings reveal that experts reinterpret values to fit local moral logics: privacy as collective and relational rather than individual; transparency as trust-building accountability rather than technical disclosure; and fairness as equity in access and representation rather than parity in outcomes. We identify these as translation gaps between encoded global frameworks and situated local practices. Finally, we propose pathways toward plural governance that redistributes epistemic authority and treats ethical negotiation as an ongoing, context-sensitive process rather than a settled technical standard.

</details>


### [237] [A Temporal Planning Approach for Intelligent Flood Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20510)
*Fazlul Hasan Siddiqui,Md. Monjurul Islam,Sabah Binte Noor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于时间规划（temporal planning）的智能洪水响应框架，该框架建模洪水响应的完整操作生命周期，涵盖优先级驱动的分诊、路线可达性与旅行成本、资源分配和供应管理，并支持执行中的重新规划。框架同时用ANML和PDDL 2.1两种语言表述，实验结果验证了其可行性和可扩展性，并为规划器选择提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面对多区域同时被淹的紧急情况，需要在严重资源约束下按正确时间顺序协调各项行动，这是一个具有挑战性的问题。自动化规划通过生成时间感知的调度方案，为这一挑战提供了解决基础，但需要一个完整的框架来建模洪水响应的全生命周期。

Method: 该框架利用时间规划技术，将优先级驱动的分诊、路线可达性与旅行成本、资源分配和供应管理整合到统一模型中，并支持执行中途遇到环境突变时进行重新规划。框架分别用ANML和PDDL 2.1两种动作建模语言表述，以兼容更多时间规划器。

Result: 实验结果证明了该框架的可行性和可扩展性，表明洪水响应场景可以有效地用时间规划进行建模和求解，并提供了不同规划器的选择指导。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了时间规划在灾害响应领域的实际应用价值，通过双语言建模提高了框架的兼容性和通用性，为实际洪水应急调度提供了一种智能化的决策支持方案。

Abstract: Effective response to multiple, simultaneously flooded areas requires coordinating appropriate actions in the correct temporal order, under severe resource constraints. Automated planning provides a foundation for addressing this challenge by generating time-aware schedules, given a formal description of available resources, constraints, and goals. This work presents an intelligent flood-response framework that exploits temporal planning and models the complete operational life cycle of flood response. The framework incorporates priority-driven triage, route accessibility and travel costs, resource allocation, and supply management, while also supporting mid-execution re-planning in response to unexpected environmental changes. The framework is formulated both in the Action Notation Modeling Language (ANML) and the Planning Domain Definition Language (PDDL) 2.1, facilitating compatibility with a wider range of temporal planners. Experimental results establish the feasibility and scalability of the proposed framework, showing that flood response scenarios can be effectively modeled and solved using temporal planning, while providing guidance on planner selection.

</details>


### [238] [FL-MAESTRO: Multi-Agent LLM Orchestration for Resource-Constrained Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20518)
*Jiajun Wu,Zirui Wang,Jiayu Zhou,Qiang Ye,Steve Drew*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FL-MAESTRO是一个多智能体编排器，利用三个专业LLM智能体分别处理联邦学习中的通信拓扑、资源分配和聚合规则三个耦合决策，通过协调器和非LLM可行性检查确保决策可执行，在非IID CIFAR-10基准上达到最强能量感知基线的精度，同时将浪费的轮次能量从三分之一以上降至接近零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习中通信拓扑是运行时变量而非固定设计，每轮需要同时提交三个耦合决策（通信拓扑、每客户端资源分配、聚合规则）。现有智能体系统要么在设置阶段操作，要么只处理单一运行时维度（如客户端选择），缺乏对联合运行时FL决策的统一处理。

Method: 提出FL-MAESTRO，一个多智能体编排器：三个专业LLM智能体分别负责一个决策维度（通信拓扑、资源分配、聚合规则），协调器将三者分析合成为单一决策，非LLM可行性检查在执行前验证决策。编排器读取服务器的预测故障列表，拒绝其更新永远不会被聚合的客户端；客户端状态以自然文本配置文件形式读取，无需按类别的能量模型。

Result: 在非IID CIFAR-10基准上，FL-MAESTRO匹配最强能量感知基线的精度，同时将浪费的轮次能量从超过三分之一降至接近零。

Conclusion: FL-MAESTRO展示了LLM多智能体系统在联邦学习运行时联合决策中的有效性，通过剔除不会被聚合的客户端消除了经典FL在不稳定边缘网络上浪费轮次能量的主要来源，并自然扩展到异构设备类别。代码已开源。

Abstract: In Federated Learning (FL), the communication topology is a runtime variable rather than a fixed design choice, since links and edge devices drop in and out during training. Each round, the server must commit three coupled decisions, namely the communication topology, per-client resource allocation, and the aggregation rule for combining local updates. Recent agentic systems have begun bringing large language models (LLM) into FL, but the existing line of work either operates at setup time or handles a single runtime dimension such as client selection. We propose FL-MAESTRO, a multi-agent orchestrator that makes the joint runtime FL decision directly through three specialist LLM agents, one per decision dimension. A coordinator combines their analyses into a single decision, and a non-LLM feasibility check confirms it before the round executes. Because the orchestrator consumes the server's predicted-failure list, it withholds clients whose updates would never be aggregated, which removes the dominant source of wasted round energy in classical FL on volatile edge networks. Because client state is read as natural-text profiles, the same orchestrator extends to heterogeneous device classes without per-class energy models. On a non-IID CIFAR-10 benchmark, FL-MAESTRO matches the accuracy of the strongest energy-aware baseline while cutting wasted round energy from over a third to near zero. Code is available at https://github.com/denoslab/FL-MAESTRO.

</details>


### [239] [Volumetric Radiology AI in the Era of Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20549)
*Zanting Ye,Shengyuan Liu,Xin Liu,Chenhui Wang,Zhisong Wang,Jiashuai Liu,Zipei Wang,Cheng Wang,Wentao Pan,Mengjie Fang,Di Dong,Mohammad Salmanpour,Arman Rahmim,Yu Gu,Yong Xia,Hongming Shan,Yixuan Yuan,Yefeng Zheng,Lijun Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了截至2026年7月超过200篇关于多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在体素放射学中应用的研究，重点围绕体素级表示、多模态理解及智能体编排展开，并提出了一个Claim-Design-Validation框架来评估技术、工作流和临床声明是否与设计及验证相匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体素放射学存在根本性的表示不匹配问题：临床解读往往需要全容积3D空间上下文和基于采集的定量信息，而当前MLLMs通常基于选定的2D图像、压缩视觉表示或报告派生文本进行处理，这限制了体积级放射学AI的可靠性和临床可信度，因此需要系统性的综述和评估框架。

Method: 对超过200篇截至2026年7月的文献进行系统综述，按模型层面的体素表示与多模态理解、系统层面的智能体编排，以及临床应用与评估维度组织文献；引入了Claim-Design-Validation框架来检查技术、工作流和临床声明是否配以适当的设计与验证。

Result: 综述发现，原生体素建模和智能体能力的取舍取决于任务的空间、定量、上下文和工作流需求；区分了何种场景下选定2D视图或报告中介推理已足够，何时需要原生体素建模；指出临床可信度需要忠实的体素表示、可追踪的系统行为、与声明一致的验证以及真实工作流中明确的人机监督。

Conclusion: 体素放射学中的MLLM应用应依据任务需求选择适当的表示与系统架构，而非一味追求原生3D建模；临床落地需要以声明-设计-验证的严谨匹配为核心，辅以忠实表示、可追溯行为、合理验证和清晰的人机分工。

Abstract: Advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are extending radiological artificial intelligence (AI) beyond task-specific image analysis toward multimodal understanding and reasoning. Volumetric radiology, however, presents a fundamental representational mismatch: clinical interpretation often requires full-volume spatial context and acquisition-dependent quantitative information, whereas current MLLMs are commonly conditioned on selected two-dimensional (2D) images, compressed visual representations, or report-derived text. Reliable volumetric radiology AI therefore requires representations that preserve task-relevant three-dimensional (3D) information and systems that can access, verify, and integrate this information across clinical workflows. In this Review, we examine more than 200 publications through July 2026. We organize the literature around volumetric representation and multimodal understanding at the model level, agentic orchestration at the system level, and their links to clinical applications and evaluation. We review volumetric foundation models, language alignment and compression strategies, and agentic systems that extend MLLMs through planning, tools, memory, and workflow interaction. We distinguish settings in which selected 2D views or report-mediated reasoning may suffice from those that warrant native volumetric modeling. We also introduce a Claim-Design-Validation framework to assess whether technical, workflow, and clinical claims are matched by appropriate design and validation. Across the literature, native volumetric modeling and agentic capabilities depend on the spatial, quantitative, contextual, and workflow requirements of the intended task. Clinical credibility requires faithful volumetric representation, traceable system behavior, claim-aligned validation, and clearly defined human oversight in realistic workflows.

</details>


### [240] [Consilience: Conformally Calibrated Communication Control for Hidden-Profile Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20564)
*Abhijith Babu,Ramneet Kaur,Vishal Pramanik,Olivera Kotevska,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Susmit Jha,Sunny Raj,Yanzhao Wu,Sumit Kumar Jha,Anirban Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Consilience框架，一个通过逐轮共形校准（conformal calibration）来引导并认证多智能体LLM通信的推理时编排框架，在隐藏档案（hidden-profile）任务中提供分布无关的有限样本保证，确保每轮通信动作的one-step regret以至少1-α的边际概率被校准阈值约束，在12个开源和闭源语言模型上显著提升决策准确性和通信效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过汇聚不同视角可提升推理能力，但其有效性依赖于通信协调，特别是在隐藏档案场景下每个智能体只掌握部分证据。现有协议（固定调度、循环交换、非结构化辩论）无法保证对话动作的适当性，缺乏对通信过程的正式保障。

Method: Consilience在每个回合用紧凑状态（压缩不确定性、分歧、证据增益、冗余和过早共识）总结讨论，然后选择通信干预（挑战、澄清、寻求证据或路由）和合适的说话者。核心贡献是逐轮共形校准程序，提供分布无关、有限样本的保证：每轮讨论中，控制器提议动作的one-step regret以至少1-α的边际概率被校准阈值约束；接受机制通过替换不可接受的提议来强制执行相同保证。

Result: 在HiddenBench风格的隐藏档案任务上，跨越12个开源和闭源语言模型，Consilience相比固定和非结构化讨论协议提升了决策准确性和通信效率，有时甚至超过所有智能体都能观察全部证据的全信息基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明经过认证的自适应通信控制比增加信息可用性更有价值，为可靠的多智能体LLM协调提供了实用机制。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems can improve reasoning by pooling diverse perspectives, but their effectiveness depends on coordinating communication, particularly in hidden-profile settings where each agent holds only part of the evidence required for a correct decision. Existing protocols, including fixed schedules, round-robin exchange, and unstructured debate, provide no guarantee that a conversational action is appropriate. We propose Consilience, an inference-time orchestration framework that both steers and certifies multi-agent communication under distributed private information. At each turn, Consilience summarizes the discussion using a compact state capturing uncertainty, disagreement, evidence gain, redundancy, and premature consensus, then selects both a communication intervention (challenge, clarify, seek evidence, or route) and an appropriate speaker. Its central contribution is a round-wise conformal calibration procedure that provides a distribution-free, finite-sample guarantee: at each discussion round, conditional on reaching that round, the one-step regret of a controller's proposed action is bounded by a calibrated threshold with marginal probability at least 1 - alpha; an acceptance mechanism enforces the same guarantee for the executed action by replacing inadmissible proposals. On HiddenBench-style hidden-profile tasks spanning 12 open and closed weight language models, Consilience improves decision accuracy and communication efficiency over fixed and unstructured discussion protocols, sometimes surpassing a full-information baseline where every agent observes all evidence. These results demonstrate that certified adaptive communication control can be more valuable than increasing information availability, providing a practical mechanism for reliable multi-agent LLM coordination.

</details>


### [241] [Open-Weight Masked Introspection: Measuring What Language Models Can Report About Their Own Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20569)
*Emilio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过构建Open-Weight Masked Introspection (OWMI)框架，对来自七个家族的八个开源权重模型进行了超过78,000次测量，发现这些模型无法可靠地内省自身内部状态的变化：没有一个模型能在真实干预与虚假干预（sham）之间做出优于随机的区分（AUROC约0.5007），等价性检验将效应上界限制在0.15个百分点AUROC以内。然而，所有所需信息实际都存在于模型中——微调后的模型可近乎完美地恢复干预信息，线性探针可从激活中达到75%-95.8%的准确率（最后一层可达零误差）。这表明失败点在于内部状态到语言报告的通路，而非内部表征本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究声称在特定条件下，足够复杂的模型可以审计自身内部状态、报告变化并自信地反馈。作者希望验证这一声称是否成立，特别是针对开源权重模型，因为模型的自我证词是否可信对AI安全与对齐监督至关重要。

Method: 构建OWMI框架，在残差流位置、注意力头和稀疏自编码器特征上实施干预，然后询问模型关于变化的问题。设计了必须超越的三种零假设条件：未做任何改变的虚假运行、影响匹配的随机扰动、以及仅能看到可见输出的纯文本观察者。在八个开源权重模型上进行系统化测量，并辅以微调模型和线性探针作为信息存在性的对照实验。

Result: 所有模型在真实干预与虚假干预的辨别上均不优于随机（AUROC约0.5007，效应上界<0.15个百分点）。但内部信息确实存在：微调后的模型在held-out方向上近乎完美恢复，线性探针达到75%-95.8%准确率，在最后一层达到零held-out误差。一个模型中信号仅出现在置信度而非文字输出上（AUROC 0.647）。

Conclusion: 当前开源权重模型缺乏内省能力，失败点在于从内部状态到语言报告的通路。因此，依赖模型自身证词的监督机制需要对照内部参考进行验证。虽然当前模型无法内省，但未来模型的能力边界尚未定论。

Abstract: Are frontier models able to introspect about their internal states? Recent work suggests that under certain conditions a complex enough model can audit its own internals, call out what changed, and report back confidently about it. We tested that claim on eight open-weight models from seven families and found no such ability: asked whether their own computation had been altered, none answered better than chance. To test it we built Open-Weight Masked Introspection (OWMI), a framework that intervenes on residual-stream sites, attention heads and sparse-autoencoder features, then interrogates the model about the change against the null conditions an answer has to beat: sham runs where nothing was altered, impact-matched random perturbations, and a text-only observer that sees only the visible output.
  Over 78,000 measurements, no model's report discriminates a real intervention from a sham beyond chance (AUROC ~0.5007), and an equivalence test bounds the effect below 0.15 percentage points of AUROC. Surprisingly, all the information needed is in the models. A model fine-tuned to report this class of intervention reaches near-perfect recovery on held-out directions, and a linear probe recovers intervention presence from the same activations at 75% to 95.8% accuracy, sharpening to no held-out error at the last layer before the model speaks. In one model the signal surfaces in the confidence rather than the words: its yes-or-no report never varies, while the confidence attached to it separates intervention from sham at AUROC 0.647. The failure sits in the path from internal state to verbal report, so oversight that reads a model's own testimony needs validating against an internal reference.
  While our results show the inability of current open-weight models to introspect, the debate is not settled for future models.

</details>


### [242] [FlavourBench: Ranking Frontier Language Models with Executable Culinary Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20574)
*Josef Chen,Erim Hayretci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlavourBench 是一个自动化的开放式语言模型基准测试，通过"风味系统"提供密集、可执行的真实标准。在534个核心任务上评估27个前沿模型端点，任务要求从8种配料中选择3种配料组合。该基准通过消除差分缺失、采用严格的自举法和符号翻转检验来控制统计推断，并附带完整的可复现资源和离线验证器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式语言模型基准测试通常依赖不稳定的评判者（人类偏好、判分模型或脆弱的精确匹配键），缺乏可靠、可执行且可完全复现的地面真值。FlavourBench旨在通过引入版本化的烹饪系统来提供密集、可执行的地面真值，使模型评估更加客观和可验证。

Method: 每个任务包含8种配料，要求模型给出3种配料的组合；在执行前，Epicure系统对所有56种可能组合进行评分。评估了27个前沿模型端点在534个相同核心任务上的表现（涵盖替换、配对和约束组合）。每个排名模型在每个面板和家族中恰好有89个有效响应（共14,418个模型-任务单元），消除了排行榜中的差分缺失。FlavourBench Score为冻结任务分数的等家族均值。使用50,000个锚簇自举重复进行95%同步置信带，100,000次符号翻转抽样覆盖351对模型对比，并采用Holm校正控制多重比较。

Result: 两个独立编制的面板相关系数r=0.89（秩相关rho=0.80）。Grok 4.6取得最大点估计65.1（同步95% CI为61.0-69.2）；351对模型对比中有101对达到显著差异。

Conclusion: FlavourBench提供了一种无需人工评判的自动化、可执行地面真值的基准测试方法，具有完整的可复现性（包含提示、评分映射、原始响应、精确路径、内容哈希和离线验证器）。两个独立面板的高一致性验证了基准的可靠性。

Abstract: Open-ended language-model benchmarks usually inherit a judge: a human preference panel, another model, or a brittle exact-match key. We introduce FlavourBench, an automated benchmark in which a versioned culinary system supplies dense, executable ground truth. Each task presents eight ingredients and asks for a three-ingredient portfolio; before model execution, Epicure scores all 56 possible portfolios.
